([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/permutation-based-meta-analysis-pvalue-estimation))
- **permutation-based-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when comparing pathway activity levels between two sample groups with per-pathway t statistics and a family-wise error correction is needed for many correlated pathway tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-based-multiple-testing-correction))
- **permutation-based-null-overlap-estimation**: Use when multiple DA tools have been run independently on several datasets of the same disease/environment and you need to test whether the overlap of significant genera exceeds chance (consistency analysis). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/permutation-based-null-overlap-estimation))
- **permutation-based-pathway-significance-testing**: Use when a gene-by-sample matrix has been converted to pathway activity levels for ~400 KEGG/Biocarta gene-sets and you need empirical p-values for phenotype associations without parametric distributional assumptions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-based-pathway-significance-testing))
- **permutation-based-pc-number-selection**: Use when a scRNAseq pipeline needs to decide how many PCs to retain before clustering, and the choice must be justified by a shuffled-data null instead of an arbitrary elbow/scree cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-pc-number-selection))
- **permutation-based-prioritization-benchmarking**: Use when you have a ranked per-patient candidate gene list with a known causative gene and need to quantify whether the prioritization rate exceeds chance, e.g. reproducing metPropagate's 92% top-20th-percentile rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-based-prioritization-benchmarking))
- **permutation-based-pvalue-computation**: Use when running mMeta.aMeta on study-by-marker summary statistics (regression coefficient estimates and standard errors) and hypothesis-testing p-values are needed across multiple related markers, particularly when marker-by-marker correlations are unknown. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/permutation-based-pvalue-computation))
- **permutation-based-pvalue-estimation**: Use when testing many predefined gene-sets (~400 KEGG/Biocarta pathways) for differential activity between two sample groups and you need a multiple-testing-aware empirical p-value without parametric assumptions on small microarray cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-based-pvalue-estimation))
- **permutation-based-pvalue-estimation**: Use when a microbiome association test statistic (e.g. SPU/aSPU/AMAT T_AMAT) has no tractable analytic null distribution and an empirical P-value must be calibrated by permuting the phenotype vector. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/permutation-based-pvalue-estimation))
- **permutation-based-rarefaction-analysis**: Use when you have a MOTU-by-sample detection table from metabarcoding and need to test whether local taxonomic richness has saturated or whether more sampling effort would recover additional diversity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/permutation-based-rarefaction-analysis))
- **permutation-based-signature-thresholding**: Use when merging multiple cell-type-specific senescence hub signatures (e.g., all human or mouse hubs in hubs.metadata) into a universal signature and you need an automatic, statistically grounded overlap_threshold instead of hand-picking one. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-signature-thresholding))
- **permutation-based-significance-testing**: Use when regressing CCLE pharmacogenomic drug response (e.g. PLX4720 sensitivity) against regulation-potential (RP) scores of TR targets and you need empirical significance beyond parametric uniP/multiP likelihood-ratio tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-significance-testing--79cb9a12100563be))
- **permutation-based-significance-testing**: Use when a positional enrichment profile (one correlation per heatmap column relative to an anchor point such as a TSS or motif) must carry statistical significance and confidence bands robust to GC bias and spatial autocorrelation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-significance-testing--c2af1ab804670698))
- **permutation-based-significance-testing**: Use when a pathway-level importance statistic (MB-VIP, VIP, RGCCA loading relevance, bootstrap non-zero-loading count) needs a p-value, and no closed-form null exists because the statistic comes from a fitted multivariate model (MB-PLS, PLS, DIABLO). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-based-significance-testing--bb50591411f87f09))
- **permutation-based-significance-testing**: Use when comparing mean pathway activity levels between two sample groups (e.g., diabetic vs. non-diabetic, current vs. never smokers) and you need a p-value that controls for multiple testing across ~400 pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-based-significance-testing--f70524ca9dd68292))
- **permutation-based-significance-testing**: Use when you need to test which scETM-learned topics are differentially abundant between labeled cell groups (e.g., AD vs control, suicidal vs healthy, or across cell types) and require nonparametric p-values with multiple-testing control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-significance-testing--bdf43fa090ba86d2))
- **permutation-based-significance-testing**: Use when you must test whether gene signatures (e.g., age-dynamic senescence genes in a cell type) exceed what null label permutations would produce, or set a data-driven overlap cutoff when merging SenePy hubs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-significance-testing--50d49dd56d602cf6))
- **permutation-based-significance-testing**: Use when benchmarking GRN inference methods (e.g., SCING, GRNBOOST2, PIDC, ppcor) on Perturb-seq data where significant downstream targets of guides must be identified to compute AUROC and TPR at FPR 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-significance-testing--cbbd1a79dc54f0b4))
- **permutation-based-significance-testing-of-hidden-factors**: Use when running IA-SVA (or validating a reimplementation) on scRNA-seq read counts and you must decide whether an extracted PC1/SV is significant, or how many hidden factors (SVs) to retain via the significance index (SI). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-significance-testing-of-hidden-factors))
- **permutation-based-significance-thresholding**: Use when running mixed-model GWAS across many variant-trait pairs (e.g. 68 SVs, 139,396 SNPs, 22,058 indels vs. quantitative traits) and you need empirical, trait-specific significance thresholds instead of a generic genome-wide P-value cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/permutation-based-significance-thresholding))
- **permutation-based-threshold-calculation**: Use when merging multiple SenePy senescence signatures (e.g., all mouse or human cell-type hubs) into a universal signature and you need an automatic, statistically grounded overlap threshold instead of an arbitrary overlap_threshold count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-threshold-calculation))
- **permutation-based-threshold-testing**: Use when screening high-dimensional OTU/count features for outcome association by permuting an adjusted response (B = 500) to build per-feature distance-correlation nulls and select a data-driven testing subset S. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/permutation-based-threshold-testing))
- **permutation-based-topology-testing**: Use when single-cell data from multiple conditions must be fitted to trajectories and you must decide, as Step 1 of the condiments workflow, whether trajectories differ fundamentally between conditions (differential topology). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-based-topology-testing))
- **permutation-control-statistics**: Use when evaluating whether BIRD-predicted DNase I hypersensitivity correlations in held-out cell types reflect real expression–accessibility coupling, by benchmarking prediction-truth rL and rC against permuted-model controls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-control-statistics))
- **permutation-cross-validation-design**: Use when validating a supervised PLS-DA discriminant model built on lipid molecular species (LMS) intensity data and you need permutation-based cross-validation statistics (Accuracy, R2Y, Q2Y) per PLS component to assess classification robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-cross-validation-design))
- **permutation-enrichment-testing**: Use when a set of significant events (e.g., ASAS SNPs at FDR &lt;= 10%) needs to be tested for enrichment of overlap with an external annotation (e.g., GWAS catalog SNPs) against a matched random null. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-enrichment-testing))
- **permutation-entropy-artifact-robust-eeg**: Use on raw EEG; ordinal entropy survives blink artifacts. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/physics-math/permutation-entropy-artifact-robust-eeg) · ★4)
- **permutation-invariant-qec-recovery**: Quantum Error Recovery (QER) methodology for permutation-invariant (PI) quantum codes under correlated noise. Uses channel-aware recovery maps with tunable PI code parameters to achieve fidelity beyond noise-independent QEC. Covers CAD code family construction, coherent recovery circuit compilation, and low-overhead implementation. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/quantum/permutation-invariant-qec-recovery) · ★4)
- **permutation-matrix-optimization**: Use when a weighted cluster interlacement graph (CI-graph) of cell-type spatial relationships and a color-difference target matrix (CD-graph) must be matched to assign contrasting colors to neighboring cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-matrix-optimization))
- **permutation-null-approximation**: Use when testing many metabolite hypotheses (e.g., biomarker discovery) and a standard single-statistic FDR is inflated; input includes observed t statistics (z1) with their log standard errors (z2) plus permutation-based null statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-null-approximation))
- **permutation-null-count-data-simulation**: Use when benchmarking co-expression estimators or p-value calibration on scRNA-seq data: you need null UMI count matrices with realistic depth variation but zero true co-expression, e.g. to reproduce bias curves or type-I error Q-Q plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-null-count-data-simulation))
- **permutation-null-distribution-analysis**: Use when testing whether two transcriptome region sets (e.g., m6A RNA methylation sites and stop codon regions) colocalize more than expected by chance, especially when region-to-isoform association is ambiguous. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-null-distribution-analysis))
- **permutation-null-distribution-significance-testing**: Use when pairwise MS/MS spectral alignments (e.g. from SIMILE max-weight matching) need p-value-based significance calls so candidate compound pairs can be ranked and compared against cosine-based scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-null-distribution-significance-testing))
- **permutation-null-hypothesis-testing**: Use when you must demonstrate that an observed cell-to-cell similarity/distance metric (e.g., ACTIONet JSD similarity) exceeds random expectation, by comparing it against a label-permuted null and a competing baseline metric (e.g., Euclidean distance on HVG+PCA coordinates). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-null-hypothesis-testing))
- **permutation-null-pvalue-gamma-modeling**: Use when enrichment p-values from Fisher's exact tests on network module/pathway hits need a permutation-estimated null because the small-of overlap-corrected overlap count is non-standard. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-null-pvalue-gamma-modeling))
- **permutation-null-sampling**: Use when pathway activity levels (e.g. the first metacompound/SVD singular vector per metabolite set) have been computed and calibrated p-values are needed that account for noise and missing peaks in metabolomics intensity data, for comparisons like Stage_1/Control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-null-sampling))
- **permutation-null-significance-testing**: Use when validating a longitudinal tool's false-positive control on semi-synthetic or real count/proportion data by shuffling the continuous time variable within each individual, as done for the fasting stool microbiome benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/permutation-null-significance-testing))
- **permutation-null-significance-testing**: Use when detected network modules (e.g. fRNC global/local RNCs from ESCA TCGA data) must be validated against a random null to confirm their scores significantly exceed chance at the detection FDR threshold (1e-5). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-null-significance-testing))
- **permutation-null-zscore-statistics**: Use when you need to quantify whether colocalization between two transcriptome region sets (e.g. miCLIP-seq m6A sites and stop codon regions) is statistically significant, via a permutation-derived null distribution, p-value, z-score and shifted z-score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-null-zscore-statistics))
- **permutation-result-visualization**: Use when a permutation test from RgnTX (permTestTx, permTestTx_customPick, or permTestTxIA_customPick) has produced a null overlap distribution and you need to plot observed vs. random overlaps, p-values, or z-scores for a transcriptome colocalization result. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-result-visualization))
- **permutation-shuffling-null-generation**: Use when benchmarking null-range generation for genomic overlap testing: you need a shuffling/permutation baseline null distribution of overlap rates to compare against block bootstrapping (bootRanges) for FPR calibration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/permutation-shuffling-null-generation))
- **permutation-significance-testing**: Use when a TF–pathway interaction score from time-series expression data needs an empirical significance call: TSMiner derives each block's probability via permutation testing and accepts interactions with score &gt;= 50 and probability &gt;= 50%. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/permutation-significance-testing))
- **permutation-significance-testing**: Use when you have trained the SARTRE random forest classifier (e.g. on stitch_yeast with shadow-price or 128-bit fingerprint features) and need to verify that observed AUC/metrics are statistically significant rather than artifacts of label structure or feature information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-significance-testing--e4948e3313787114))
- **permutation-significance-testing**: Use when correlating metabolomics-derived substructure motifs (Mass2Motifs) with genome-mined sub-cluster features across a paired genome-metabolome strain panel, and you need p-values that account for non-independence of closely related strains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-significance-testing--ac5d79b2f7faf714))
- **permutation-significance-testing**: Use when you must attach an empirical significance level to an entropy- or potency-derived statistic — e.g. testing whether a bulk-sample signalling entropy (SR) significantly exceeds a matched single-cell SR distribution — without assuming a parametric null. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-significance-testing--b14e4533dab5f37b))
- **permutation-significance-testing**: Use when a NeuronChat run on scRNA-seq or spatial transcriptomics must identify which sender–receiver communication links are significant (p &lt; 0.05) before aggregating networks, ranking interactions, or comparing conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-significance-testing--173a8a5f92bff321))
- **permutation-significance-testing-of-communication-links**: Use when inferred ligand-receptor communication networks from scRNA-seq (NeuronChat objects) must be filtered for statistical significance, or when assessing stability of significant-link calls across permutation counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-significance-testing-of-communication-links))
- **permutation-snr-representation-invariance**: Use when probing why models fail equivalent problems or analyzing internal representations of reasoning. Permutation SNR metric distinguishing answer invariance from representation invariance; higher SNR correlates with accuracy on reordered problems. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/permutation-snr-representation-invariance) · ★4)
- **permutation-stability-comparison-analysis**: Use when running NeuronChat's run_NeuronChat permutation test and you must verify that significance calls at a modest M (e.g., 100) are stable and consistent with a large-M reference run (e.g., 1000). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-stability-comparison-analysis))
- **permutation-test-group-label-shuffling**: Use when filtering inferred ligand-receptor interactions in scRNA-seq cell-cell communication analysis, i.e., after computing communication probabilities and needing a nonparametric null model to call interactions significant at p &lt; 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-test-group-label-shuffling))
- **permutation-test-model-validation**: Use when a supervised classifier (OPLS-DA, RF) fitted on a feature table where features vastly outnumber samples reports high training fit (R²Y) and you must establish whether it generalizes, via k-fold CV and permutation testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-test-model-validation))
- **permutation-test-p-value-estimation**: Use when after computing Multi-Block Variable Importance in Projection (MB-VIP) scores on a fitted MB-PLS discriminant model, when you need to distinguish signal features from noise by establishing empirical significance thresholds rather than relying on parametric assumptions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/permutation-test-p-value-estimation) · 3 copies)
- **permutation-testing-empirical-pvalue-estimation**: Use when a fitted multiblock PLS model (e.g. MamsiPls on multi-assay LC-MS feature tables) needs statistically defensible per-feature significance, and MB-VIP scores alone cannot distinguish signal from chance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-testing-empirical-pvalue-estimation))
- **permutation-testing-feature-significance**: Use when you have a fitted MB-PLS model over multiple LC-MS assay blocks (e.g. HPOS/LPOS/LNEG) and need statistically defensible feature significance beyond raw MB-VIP magnitudes before biomarker annotation or structural search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/permutation-testing-feature-significance))
- **permutation-testing-of-cell-group-interactions**: Use when you have a CellChat object with overexpressed signaling genes across cell groups and need to determine which predicted ligand-receptor interactions are statistically significant rather than chance-level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-testing-of-cell-group-interactions))
- **permutation-testing-of-clonal-state-enrichment**: Use when single-cell time-course data has CellTag lineage barcodes and a fate label (e.g. Capybara-assigned iEP conversion), and you must split cells at an intermediate time point into clone groups enriched or depleted for successful fate conversion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/permutation-testing-of-clonal-state-enrichment))
- **permutation_embedding_contracts**: Check mask-preserving permutations and derive embedding groups for graphically structured diffusion models. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/task-oriented/PaperBench/skills/all-in-one/skill/graphically_structured_diffusion_models/permutation_embedding_contracts) · ★357)
- **permutational-multivariate-anova-on-distance-matrices**: Use when sample groupings must be tested for significant differences in multivariate dispersion (e.g. allele-frequency or community profiles) and only per-sample distance matrices — not raw feature tables — are available for group-wise hypothesis testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/permutational-multivariate-anova-on-distance-matrices))
- **permutationinvarianttraining**: Compute the PermutationInvariantTraining metric — provided by torchmetrics. Use when the user has predictions and ground-truth and needs to compute PermutationInvariantTraining, or asks how to score with PermutationInvariantTraining. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/permutationinvarianttraining) · ★3)
- **permuted-cluster-background-statistics**: Use when validating de novo motif discovery on MAPIT-seq edit clusters: you need a matched control background (permuted clusters in the same genes) to compute fold enrichment of a known motif such as UGCAUG against HOMER output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/permuted-cluster-background-statistics))
- **pern-stack**: Build, review, debug, and improve full-stack applications using PostgreSQL, Express.js, React, and Node.js with maintainable and production-ready practices. ([rafidstudio/agent-skills](https://github.com/rafidstudio/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/pern-stack) · ★3)
- **pernambuques**: Use quando pedirem para reescrever um texto pronto "em pernambuquês", "deixar pernambucano", dar voz recifense, sotaque de Pernambuco, gírias do Recife, ou um "pernambucano formal/culto" em copy, push, e-mail, marketing, diálogo de personagem ou mensagem; também quando o usuário digitar /pernambuques girias ou /pernambuques formal. ([magnogouveia/pernambuques](https://github.com/magnogouveia/pernambuques/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
